| RK-10.6.1-010 | behavior | must | untested: This sets what the backend does not carry and obliges nothing a scenario can see: a speech-to-speech channel's model holds the conversation in the owner's process, so it has no intelligence turn for a backend to carry, and the fan-out of an event to another process's live connections belongs to a deployment. | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-011 | behavior | must | untested: The serialized messages a backend carries lie below the public API a scenario drives: no step reads or writes a link message, which a backend's own tests do. The same scenarios run on the in-memory and a distributed backend show only that the outcomes agree. | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-012 | behavior | must | untested: Whether the in-memory backend serializes each message is not observable through the public API: a turn's output reads the same either way; what a scenario can see of it, an error rebuilt by its class, is RK-10.6.8-043's. | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-013 | behavior | must | untested: That nothing changes a request's events in place is a property of the language objects the owner and the executor hold: no scripted hook or channel changes a record in place, and a hook's modify returns a new one. | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-014 | behavior | may | — | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-015 | behavior | must | untested: Whether an implementation keeps a local shortcut to the channel's objects is its internal structure: a scenario sees the turn's outcome, which a shortcut and the backend give alike. | | | |
| RK-10.6.1-016 | behavior | must | untested: Holds of the suite as a whole, not of one scenario: every scenario of an intelligence channel runs through the in-memory backend, and their receipts are the evidence. | | | |

| Unit | Kind | Level | Scenario | Lock | Python | Rust |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RK-10.6.9-001 | behavior | must | — | | | |
| RK-10.6.9-005 | behavior | must | untested: Holds of the suite as a whole, not of one scenario: whether an executor holds a store is how the host builds it, which no step sets, and every executors scenario runs on executors the runners build without one. | | | |
| RK-10.6.9-006 | behavior | must | untested: The lock manager several owner processes share is a deployment prerequisite, and that an executor takes no room lock is its internal structure: a scenario sees the owner's lock rules, which the units of Section 13.5 state. | | | |
